{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Rebased\\README.md: # Rebased: Hazard Trigger Full-Stack Reconstruction\n\n## Overview\n\nRebased is a standalone full-stack project that merges the **Hazard Trigger** gacha-driven prompt engineering system with **Rose's** 5-tier SQLite architecture. It provides a deterministic, zero-LLM approach to AI art prompt generation with weighted node sampling and preset management.\n\n**Status**: Ready for development\n\n## Quick Start\n\n### Prerequisites\n\n- Python 3.8+\n- Node.js 18+\n- npm or yarn\n\n### Installation\n\n```bash\n# Backend\ncd backend\npip install -r requirements.txt\n\n# Frontend\ncd frontend\nnpm install\n```\n\n### Development\n\n```bash\n# Windows\n./run.bat\n\n# Linux/Mac\n./run.sh\n```\n\nFrontend opens at `http://localhost:5173`\nBackend API at `http://localhost:20140`\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Backend (FastAPI + SQLite)\n\n- **Port**: 20140\n- **Database**: SQLite with WAL mode, IMMEDIATE transactions\n- **Schema**: 5-tier relational (categories, nodes, pools, presets, wildcards)\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Deterministic graph resolver (zero LLM)\n  - Weighted random sampling for gacha mechanics\n  - REST API for full CRUD operations\n  - Graceful error handling and logging\n\n### Frontend (React 18 + TypeScript + Vite)\n\n- **Port**: 5173\n- **State Management**: Zustand with Immer middleware\n- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with custom hazard theme\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Real-time node matrix grid with search/filter\n  - Preset management and loading\n  - Output panel with prompt assembly\n  - Gacha pool inspector\n  - Dark theme with theme-aware tokens\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\nRebased/\n├── backend/\n│   ├── main.py                 # FastAPI server\n│   ├── db.py                   # 5-tier SQLite schema\n│   ├── models.py               # Pydantic models\n│   ├── graph_resolver.py       # Deterministic resolver\n│   ├── seed_data.py            # Database initialization\n│   └── requirements.txt\n├── frontend/\n│   ├── src/\n│   │   ├── app/                # React components\n│   │   ├── store/              # Zustand store\n│   │   ├── types/              # TypeScript definitions\n│   │   ├── styles/             # Global styles\n│   │   └── main.tsx\n│   ├── index.html\n│   ├── package.json\n│   ├── vite.config.ts\n│   ├── tsconfig.json\n│   └── tailwind.config.js\n├── run.bat                      # Windows launcher\n├── run.sh                       # POSIX launcher\n└── README.md\n```\n\n## API Endpoints\n\n### Categories\n- `GET /api/categories` - List all\n- `POST /api/categories` - Create\n- `DELETE /api/categories/{id}` - Delete\n\n### Nodes\n- `GET /api/nodes` - List (optional filter by category_id)\n- `POST /api/nodes` - Create\n- `PUT /api/nodes/{id}` - Update\n- `DELETE /api/nodes/{id}` - Delete\n\n### Pools\n- `GET /api/pools` - List all\n- `POST /api/pools` - Create\n- `DELETE /api/pools/{id}` - Delete\n\n### Presets\n- `GET /api/presets` - List all\n- `POST /api/presets` - Create\n- `DELETE /api/presets/{id}` - Delete\n\n### Graph Resolution\n- `POST /api/graph/resolve?preset_id=<id>&seed=<seed>` - Resolve prompt graph\n\n## Database Schema\n\n### Tables\n1. **categories** (0002...): Node groupings with hierarchy\n2. **nodes** (0001...): Prompt nodes with weights and rarity\n3. **pools** (0003...): Gacha pools with weighted items\n4. **presets** (0004...): Saved configurations\n5. **wildcards** (0005...): Token replacement patterns (future)\n\n### Key Properties\n- Hex IDs with prefixes for fast type identification\n- Foreign key constraints with cascade deletes\n- JSON fields for flexible data structures\n- WAL mode for concurrent access\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n### Hex ID Format\n- 16-character uppercase hex: `<4-char prefix><12-char unix ms>`\n- Prefixes: 0001=nodes, 0002=categories, 0003=pools, 0004=presets, 0005=wildcards\n\n### Gacha System\n- Weighted random sampling without replacement\n- Per-node default weights\n- Gacha pool draw counts\n- Wildcard expansion in tokens\n\n### Graph Resolution\n- Deterministic algorithm (seeded random)\n- Active node selection\n- Weight modifier application\n- Exclusion rule enforcement\n- LoRA extraction\n- Prompt assembly with weights\n\n## Development Notes\n\n### Code Style\n- Max 300 lines per file (4-tier separation)\n- Strict typing (Pydantic models + TypeScript interfaces)\n- No untyped dictionaries across layer boundaries\n- Clean folder structure with clear responsibilities\n\n### Testing\n- Backend: Manual API testing with curl/Postman\n- Frontend: Component testing with user interactions\n- Integration: End-to-end preset resolution workflow\n\n### Deployment\n- Backend: `python -m uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 20140`\n- Frontend: `npm run build` produces `dist/` directory\n\n## TODOs\n\n- [ {"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Rebased\\frontend\\src\\utils\\webVaultAdapter.ts: import { App, DataAdapter } from '../obsidian-shim';\n\nexport class WebStorageAdapter implements DataAdapter {\n  private baseUrl: string;\n\n  constructor(baseUrl = '/api/fs') {\n    this.baseUrl = baseUrl;\n  }\n\n  async exists(path: string): Promise<boolean> {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/exists?path=${encodeURIComponent(path)}`);\n      if (!res.ok) return false;\n      const data = await res.json();\n      return Boolean(data.exists);\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  async read(path: string): Promise<string> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/read?path=${encodeURIComponent(path)}`);\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to read file: ${path} (status ${res.status})`);\n    return await res.text();\n  }\n\n  async write(path: string, content: string): Promise<void> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/write`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ path, content }),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to write file: ${path} (status ${res.status})`);\n  }\n\n  async list(path: string): Promise<{ files: string[]; folders: string[] }> {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/list?path=${encodeURIComponent(path)}`);\n      if (!res.ok) return { files: [], folders: [] };\n      return await res.json();\n    } catch {\n      return { files: [], folders: [] };\n    }\n  }\n\n  async mkdir(path: string): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/mkdir`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ path }),\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.warn('[WebStorageAdapter] mkdir note:', e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async rename(oldPath: string, newPath: string): Promise<void> {\n    const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/rename`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ old_path: oldPath, new_path: newPath }),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to rename: ${oldPath} -> ${newPath}`);\n  }\n\n  async remove(path: string): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/remove?path=${encodeURIComponent(path)}`, {\n        method: 'DELETE',\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.warn('[WebStorageAdapter] remove note:', e);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport const createWebObsidianApp = (baseUrl = '/api/fs'): App => {\n  return {\n    vault: {\n      adapter: new WebStorageAdapter(baseUrl),\n    },\n  };\n};\n"}

{"AskUserQuestion":"\"What match structure should the tournament database support?\" [header: Match Format] options: (1) \"1v1 Head-to-Head Brackets (Recommended)\" — Traditional two-sided bracket (Slot 1 vs Slot 2, e.g. charA vs charB across 16F -> 8F -> 4F -> Final). Cleanest and most common for tournament brackets. (Recommended) (2) \"Flexible N-Way Matches\" — Arbitrary N-participant matches (position 1..N) per match, supporting 1v1, 3-way, 4-way battle royales, and group stages. | \"How should tournament results calculate and update a character's Hazard Level?\" [header: Hazard Rating] options: (1) \"Elo-Style Competitive Rating (Recommended)\" — Dynamic rating change where beating higher-hazard opponents grants more hazard points, and losses reduce hazard (with a 0.0 floor). (Recommended) (2) \"Logarithmic Odometer (EXP-based)\" — Characters accumulate Tournament Combat Points from wins and rounds survived, and Hazard Level is computed via y = 1.5 * ln(points + 1). Score never drops. (3) \"Fixed Milestone Bounties\" — Explicit predefined hazard bonuses per round won (e.g. +0.2 for 8F, +0.4 for 4F, +1.0 for Champion). | \"How should Character entities interface with the new tournament database?\" [header: Data Storage] options: (1) \"SQLite with Auto-Sync to JSON (Recommended)\" — SQLite holds authoritative tournament, match, and history tables; Character JSONs (A01.json) stay as the identity source with hazardLevel.hazardScore auto-synced upon match completion. (Recommended) (2) \"Full SQLite CQRS (Rose Pattern)\" — Move Characters entirely into SQLite tables (matching Rose 5-tier architecture) and compile read-only JSON projections."}

{"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\wow-i-ll-say-that-bright-blum.md: # Rebased Tournament Database & Bracket Engine — Architecture Plan\n\n## Context\nThe previous system stored tournament match logs in a flat, unindexed `tournaments.jsonl` file (`{\"date\":\"2026-09-27\",\"round\":\"8F\",\"charA\":\"A01\",\"charB\":\"A02\",\"winner\":\"A01\"}`). This structure has significant limitations:\n1. **No Bracket Topology**: Cannot represent binary elimination trees, multi-round parent/child dependencies, or slot seeding (`l1..l15`, `r1..r15`).\n2. **No Data Integrity**: Susceptible to orphaned match records, invalid character references, and out-of-order execution.\n3. **No Automatic Advancement**: Lacks stateful propagation where a match winner automatically advances to the designated parent bracket slot.\n\nFollowing the **Entropy Rose** architectural pattern, we replace `tournaments.jsonl` with an ACID-compliant, 5-tier relational SQLite model with deterministic Hex IDs, automated bracket progression, and an authentic interactive visual bracket UI derived from `Tournament 02 ~ Duality.md` and `bracket.css`.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Database Architecture (Rose Relational Pattern)\n\n### Hex ID Prefix Standards (16-character monotonic hex: `<4-prefix><12-hex-ms>`)\n- `0010` — Tournaments\n- `0011` — Tournament Matches / Bracket Slots\n- `0012` — Tournament Seed Registrations\n- `0001` — Characters / Competitors (matches existing Character Identity schema)\n\n### Schema Definition (`backend/tournament_db.py` or integrated into `backend/db.py`)\n\n```sql\n-- Table: tournaments (0010...)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournaments (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    name TEXT NOT NULL,\n    format TEXT DEFAULT 'single_elimination_32', -- 'single_elimination_16', 'single_elimination_32'\n    status TEXT DEFAULT 'draft',                  -- 'draft', 'in_progress', 'completed'\n    winner_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    notes TEXT,\n    FOREIGN KEY (winner_id) REFERENCES characters(id) ON DELETE SET NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_status ON tournaments(status);\n\n-- Table: tournament_participants (0012...)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_participants (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    character_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    seed_index INTEGER NOT NULL,              -- 1 to 32\n    initial_slot TEXT NOT NULL,               -- e.g. 'l1', 'l2', ..., 'r16'\n    FOREIGN KEY (tournament_id) REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tp_tourney ON tournament_participants(tournament_id);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tp_char ON tournament_participants(character_id);\n\n-- Table: tournament_matches (0011...)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_matches (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    round_name TEXT NOT NULL,                 -- '16F', '8F', '4F', 'FINAL', 'CHAMPION'\n    match_order INTEGER NOT NULL,             -- Global sequence index\n    position_code TEXT NOT NULL,              -- 'l1', 'l9', 'l13', 'l15', 'r1', ..., 'winner'\n    slot_a_char_id TEXT,                      -- Participant A character ID\n    slot_b_char_id TEXT,                      -- Participant B character ID\n    winner_char_id TEXT,                      -- Winner character ID\n    next_match_id TEXT,                       -- Parent match in tree\n    next_slot_target TEXT,                    -- 'slot_a' or 'slot_b' in next_match\n    completed_at TEXT,\n    FOREIGN KEY (tournament_id) REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n    FOREIGN KEY (next_match_id) REFERENCES tournament_matches(id) ON DELETE SET NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tm_tourney ON tournament_matches(tournament_id);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tm_pos ON tournament_matches(position_code);\n```\n\n### Bracket Tree Topology (32-Participant Dual-Wing Elimination)\nMatches the exact geometry from `Tournament 02 ~ Duality.md`:\n\n```\nLEFT WING                                                     RIGHT WING\nRound 1 (16F)    Round 2 (8F)    Round 3 (4F)   Final (2F)    Final (2F)    Round 3 (4F)    Round 2 (8F)    Round 1 (16F)\n[l1] ──┐                                                                                                    ┌── [r1]\n       ├─► [l9] ───┐                                                                                  ┌─── [r9] ◄─┤\n[l2] ──┘           │                                                                                  │           └── [r2]\n                   ├─► [l13] ──┐                                                          ┌── [r13] ◄─┤\n[l3] ──┐           │           │                                                          │           │           ┌── [r3]\n       ├─► [l10] ──┘           │                                                          │           └── [r10] ◄─┤\n[l4] ──┘                       ├─► [l15] ──┐                          ┌── [r15] ◄─────────┤                       └── [r4]\n                               │           │                          │                   │\n[l5] ──┐                       │           │                          │                   │                       ┌── [r5]\n       ├─► [l11] ──┐           │           │    ┌───────────────┐     │                   │           ┌── [r11] ◄─┤\n[l6] ──┘           │           │           ├───►│ GRAND CHAMPION│◄────┤                   │           │           └── [r6]\n                   ├─► [l14] ──┘                │   [WINNER]    │                         └── [r14] ◄─┤\n[l7] ──┐           │                            └───────────────┘                                     │           ┌── [r7]\n       ├─► [l12] ──┘                                                                                  └── [r12] ◄─┤\n[l8] ──┘                                                                                                          └── [r8]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Backend Progression & Resolution Engine (`backend/tournament_engine.py`)\n\n1. **Tournament Creation & Auto-Tree Generation**:\n   - `create_tournament(name, format, participant_ids)`: Generates the tournament record, seeds participants into initial slot positions (`l1..l8`, `r1..r8`), and pre-computes all match dependency tree links (`next_match_id` & `next_slot_target`).\n2. **Match Winner Declaration & Automated Cascading**:\n   - `record_match_winner(tournament_id, match_id, winner_id)`:\n     - Sets `winner_char_id` on the target match.\n     - Automatically updates the parent match (`next_match_id`), placing `winner_id` into `slot_a_char_id` or `slot_b_char_id`.\n     - If the match is the Grand Final (`l15` vs `r15`), marks the tournament `status = 'completed'` and sets `winner_id`.\n     - Executes within a SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction with WAL checkpoint.\n3. **CQRS Projection Compiler**:\n   - `compile_tournament_projection(tournament_id)`: Exports denormalized bracket graph (ready for frontend rendering without multi-table joins).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Frontend Interactive Visual Bracket Studio (`frontend/src/app/components/TournamentBracketView.tsx`)\n\n1. **CSS Coordinate Grid**:\n   - Implements the exact spatial positioning from `bracket.css` (`.l1..l8` top 5%..88%, `.l9..l12` top 10.5%..82.5%, `.l13..l14` top 21.5%..71.5%, `.l15` top 46.5%, and mirrored right wing `.r1..r15`).\n2. **SVG Dynamic Orthogonal Bracket Connectors**:\n   - High-performance SVG overlay computing center-to-center path lines (`M {x1} {y1} H {midX} V {y2} H {x2}`) with active-match glowing strokes.\n3. **Interactive Avatar Nodes**:\n   - Renders character avatars with hover cards displaying character name, series, alias, and current hazard class.\n   - Click-to-Pick outcome dialog for active matches with instant visual advance animation.\n4. **Integration**:\n   - Added as a tab `🏆 Tournaments` in the Hazard Trigger navigation bar alongside Triggers, Templates, Components, and Conditions.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Critical Files to Create / Modify\n\n| File Path | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `backend/tournament_db.py` | Relational SQLite schema, indexes, and atomic CRUD operations |\n| `backend/tournament_engine.py` | Tree generation, match winner propagation, bracket cascade |\n| `backend/main.py` | REST API endpoints (`/api/tournaments`, `/api/tournaments/{id}/match/winner`, etc.) |\n| `frontend/src/types/tournament.ts` | TypeScript schema for tournaments, matches, slots, bracket nodes |\n| `frontend/src/app/components/TournamentBracketView.tsx` | Interactive React bracket component with SVG connector overlay |\n| `frontend/src/app/components/TournamentListView.tsx` | Tournament manager list & tournament creator wizard |\n| `frontend/src/styles/bracket.css` | Bracket CSS geometry tokens and avatar animation styles |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Verification & Test Plan\n\n1. **Unit & Engine Tests (`backend/test_tournament.py`)**:\n   - Initialize 16/32-character participant pool.\n   - Generate full tournament bracket tree.\n   - Simulate round-by-round match resolutions: 16F -> 8F -> 4F -> Final.\n   - Assert parent slots receive child winners deterministically.\n   - Verify SQLite transaction rollback on invalid participant/match state.\n2. **API & Integration Tests**:\n   - Invoke `/api/tournaments` POST to create bracket.\n   - Verify `/api/tournaments/{id}` returns complete bracket tree projection.\n   - Verify Vite frontend renders bracket grid and SVG connectors at `http://localhost:5174/`.\n"}

{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Rebased\\backend\\tournament_engine.py: \"\"\"\nRebased Tournament Engine: Deterministic Bracket Topology & Auto-Progression.\nImplements the exact dual-wing tree structure from 'Tournament 02 ~ Duality.md'\nand 'bracket.css'.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport time\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional\nimport tournament_db as tdb\n\nDEFAULT_AVATAR = \"data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 100 100'%3E%3Crect width='100' height='100' fill='%231a1f3a'/%3E%3Ctext x='50' y='55' font-size='32' text-anchor='middle' fill='%23d4af37' font-family='monospace'%3E%3F%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E\"\n\n\ndef build_dual_wing_bracket(\n    tournament_id: str,\n    participants: List[Dict[str, Any]],\n    db_path: str = tdb.DEFAULT_DB_PATH\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Builds the complete 16-competitor dual-wing elimination bracket.\n    Generates participants and match slots wired with next_match_id links.\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = tdb.get_connection(db_path)\n\n    # 1. Pad or truncate participants to 16\n    padded_participants: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n    slot_names = [f\"l{i}\" for i in range(1, 9)] + [f\"r{i}\" for i in range(1, 9)]\n\n    for i in range(16):\n        initial_slot = slot_names[i]\n        if i < len(participants):\n            p = participants[i]\n            padded_participants.append({\n                \"character_id\": p.get(\"character_id\", f\"CHAR_{i+1:02d}\"),\n                \"display_name\": p.get(\"display_name\", f\"Competitor {i+1}\"),\n                \"avatar_url\": p.get(\"avatar_url\") or DEFAULT_AVATAR,\n                \"seed_index\": i + 1,\n                \"initial_slot\": initial_slot\n            })\n        else:\n            padded_participants.append({\n                \"character_id\": f\"BYE_{i+1:02d}\",\n                \"display_name\": f\"Seed {i+1}\",\n                \"avatar_url\": DEFAULT_AVATAR,\n                \"seed_index\": i + 1,\n                \"initial_slot\": initial_slot\n            })\n\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE;\")\n\n        # Save participants\n        for p in padded_participants:\n            pid = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_PARTICIPANT)\n            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO tournament_participants (\n                    id, tournament_id, character_id, seed_index, initial_slot, display_name, avatar_url\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);\n            \"\"\", (\n                pid,\n                tournament_id,\n                p[\"character_id\"],\n                p[\"seed_index\"],\n                p[\"initial_slot\"],\n                p[\"display_name\"],\n                p[\"avatar_url\"]\n            ))\n\n        # 2. Pre-generate Match IDs so we can cleanly wire parent pointers\n        # Final Match (l15 vs r15)\n        match_final_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n\n        # Semifinals (Round 4F: l13 vs l14 -> l15, r13 vs r14 -> r15)\n        match_l15_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r15_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n\n        # Quarterfinals (Round 8F: l9 vs l10 -> l13, l11 vs l12 -> l14, etc.)\n        match_l13_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_l14_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r13_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r14_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n\n        # Round of 16 (Base Matches: l1 vs l2 -> l9, l3 vs l4 -> l10, etc.)\n        match_l9_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_l10_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_l11_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_l12_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n\n        match_r9_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r10_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r11_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n        match_r12_id = tdb.generate_tournament_hex_id(tdb.PREFIX_MATCH)\n\n        # 3. Create Grand Final Match (order 15)\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, 'FINAL', 15, 'champion', NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);\n        \"\"\", (match_final_id, tournament_id))\n\n        # 4. Create Round 4F Matches (Semis -> Winner advances to Grand Final)\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '4F', 13, 'l15', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_a');\n        \"\"\", (match_l15_id, tournament_id, match_final_id))\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '4F', 14, 'r15', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_b');\n        \"\"\", (match_r15_id, tournament_id, match_final_id))\n\n        # 5. Create Round 8F Matches (Quarters -> Advance to l15 / r15)\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '8F', 9, 'l13', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_a');\n        \"\"\", (match_l13_id, tournament_id, match_l15_id))\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '8F', 10, 'l14', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_b');\n        \"\"\", (match_l14_id, tournament_id, match_l15_id))\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '8F', 11, 'r13', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_a');\n        \"\"\", (match_r13_id, tournament_id, match_r15_id))\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                slot_a_char_id, slot_b_char_id, next_match_id, next_slot_target\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, '8F', 12, 'r14', NULL, NULL, ?, 'slot_b');\n        \"\"\", (match_r14_id, tournament_id, match_r15_id))\n\n        # 6. Create Round 16F Base Matches (R16 -> Advance to l13, l14, r13, r14)\n        # Seed initial competitors into slots\n        p = padded_participants\n\n        # Left Wing Base Matches\n        base_match_configs = [\n            (match_l9_id, '16F', 1, 'l9', p[0], p[1], match_l13_id, 'slot_a'),\n            (match_l10_id, '16F', 2, 'l10', p[2], p[3], match_l13_id, 'slot_b'),\n            (match_l11_id, '16F', 3, 'l11', p[4], p[5], match_l14_id, 'slot_a'),\n            (match_l12_id, '16F', 4, 'l12', p[6], p[7], match_l14_id, 'slot_b'),\n\n            # Right Wing Base Matches\n            (match_r9_id, '16F', 5, 'r9', p[8], p[9], match_r13_id, 'slot_a'),\n            (match_r10_id, '16F', 6, 'r10', p[10], p[11], match_r13_id, 'slot_b'),\n            (match_r11_id, '16F', 7, 'r11', p[12], p[13], match_r14_id, 'slot_a'),\n            (match_r12_id, '16F', 8, 'r12', p[14], p[15], match_r14_id, 'slot_b'),\n        ]\n\n        for (mid, rnd, ord_num, pos, sa, sb, parent_mid, pslot) in base_match_configs:\n            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO tournament_matches (\n                    id, tournament_id, round_name, match_order, position_code,\n                    slot_a_char_id, slot_a_name, slot_a_avatar,\n                    slot_b_char_id, slot_b_name, slot_b_avatar,\n                    next_match_id, next_slot_target\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);\n            \"\"\", (\n                mid, tournament_id, rnd, ord_num, pos,\n                sa[\"character_id\"], sa[\"display_name\"], sa[\"avatar_url\"],\n                sb[\"character_id\"], sb[\"display_name\"], sb[\"avatar_url\"],\n                parent_mid, pslot\n            ))\n\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);\")\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef record_match_winner(\n    tournament_id: str,\n    match_id: str,\n    winner_char_id: str,\n    db_path: str = tdb.DEFAULT_DB_PATH\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Records the winner of a match and automatically advances the winner\n    into the designated slot of the parent match in the bracket tree.\n    If the match was the Grand Final, completes the tournament.\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = tdb.get_connection(db_path)\n    now_str = time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE;\")\n\n        # Fetch current match\n        cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM tournament_matches\n            WHERE id = ? AND tournament_id = ?;\n        \"\"\", (match_id, tournament_id))\n        match = cur.fetchone()\n        if not match:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Match {match_id} not found in tournament {tournament_id}\")\n\n        # Resolve winner details\n        if match[\"slot_a_char_id\"] == winner_char_id:\n            winner_name = match[\"slot_a_name\"]\n            winner_avatar = match[\"slot_a_avatar\"]\n        elif match[\"slot_b_char_id\"] == winner_char_id:\n            winner_name = match[\"slot_b_name\"]\n            winner_avatar = match[\"slot_b_avatar\"]\n        else:\n            # Fallback to participant lookup\n            p_cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                SELECT display_name, avatar_url FROM tournament_participants\n                WHERE tournament_id = ? AND character_id = ?;\n            \"\"\", (tournament_id, winner_char_id))\n            p_row = p_cur.fetchone()\n            winner_name = p_row[\"display_name\"] if p_row else winner_char_id\n            winner_avatar = p_row[\"avatar_url\"] if p_row else DEFAULT_AVATAR\n\n        # Update current match\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            UPDATE tournament_matches\n            SET winner_char_id = ?, winner_name = ?, winner_avatar = ?, completed_at = ?\n            WHERE id = ?;\n        \"\"\", (winner_char_id, winner_name, winner_avatar, now_str, match_id))\n\n        # Propagate into next match if exists\n        next_match_id = match[\"next_match_id\"]\n        next_slot_target = match[\"next_slot_target\"]\n\n        if next_match_id and next_slot_target:\n            if next_slot_target == \"slot_a\":\n                conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                    UPDATE tournament_matches\n                    SET slot_a_char_id = ?, slot_a_name = ?, slot_a_avatar = ?\n                    WHERE id = ?;\n                \"\"\", (winner_char_id, winner_name, winner_avatar, next_match_id))\n            elif next_slot_target == \"slot_b\":\n                conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                    UPDATE tournament_matches\n                    SET slot_b_char_id = ?, slot_b_name = ?, slot_b_avatar = ?\n                    WHERE id = ?;\n                \"\"\", (winner_char_id, winner_name, winner_avatar, next_match_id))\n\n        # If Grand Final, mark tournament completed\n        if match[\"round_name\"] == \"FINAL\":\n            conn.execute(\"\"\"\n                UPDATE tournaments\n                SET status = 'completed', winner_id = ?, winner_name = ?, updated_at = ?\n                WHERE id = ?;\n            \"\"\", (winner_char_id, winner_name, now_str, tournament_id))\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            UPDATE tournaments SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?;\n        \"\"\", (now_str, tournament_id))\n\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);\")\n    conn.close()\n\n    return get_tournament_projection(tournament_id, db_path)\n\n\ndef get_tournament_projection(\n    tournament_id: str,\n    db_path: str = tdb.DEFAULT_DB_PATH\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns the complete bracket tree projection, denormalized and ready\n    for instant rendering by the frontend bracket component.\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = tdb.get_connection(db_path)\n\n    # 1. Fetch tournament\n    t_cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM tournaments WHERE id = ?;\", (tournament_id,))\n    t_row = t_cur.fetchone()\n    if not t_row:\n        conn.close()\n        raise ValueError(f\"Tournament {tournament_id} not found\")\n    tournament_data = dict(t_row)\n\n    # 2. Fetch participants\n    p_cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT * FROM tournament_participants\n        WHERE tournament_id = ?\n        ORDER BY seed_index ASC;\n    \"\"\", (tournament_id,))\n    participants = [dict(r) for r in p_cur.fetchall()]\n\n    # 3. Fetch matches\n    m_cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT * FROM tournament_matches\n        WHERE tournament_id = ?\n        ORDER BY match_order ASC;\n    \"\"\", (tournament_id,))\n    matches = [dict(r) for r in m_cur.fetchall()]\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # 4. Build coordinate-mapped slot index for bracket.css\n    # .l1 .. .l8: Base seeds Left\n    # .r1 .. .r8: Base seeds Right\n    # .l9 .. .l12: Round of 16 winners Left\n    # .r9 .. .r12: Round of 16 winners Right\n    # .l13 .. .l14: Round of 8 winners Left\n    # .r13 .. .r14: Round of 8 winners Right\n    # .l15: Left Champion / Finalist\n    # .r15: Right Champion / Finalist\n    # champion: Grand Champion\n\n    slots_map: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n\n    # Seed participants into base slots\n    for p in participants:\n        slot_code = p[\"initial_slot\"]\n        slots_map[slot_code] = {\n            \"character_id\": p[\"character_id\"],\n            \"display_name\": p[\"display_name\"],\n            \"avatar_url\": p[\"avatar_url\"],\n            \"seed_index\": p[\"seed_index\"],\n            \"is_winner\": False\n        }\n\n    # Populate intermediate and final slots from matches\n    for m in matches:\n        pos = m[\"position_code\"]\n        if m[\"winner_char_id\"]:\n            slots_map[pos] = {\n                \"character_id\": m[\"winner_char_id\"],\n                \"display_name\": m[\"winner_name\"],\n                \"avatar_url\": m[\"winner_avatar\"],\n                \"match_id\": m[\"id\"],\n                \"is_winner\": True\n            }\n\n    return {\n        \"tournament\": tournament_data,\n        \"participants\": participants,\n        \"matches\": matches,\n        \"slots_map\": slots_map\n    }\n"}

const TournamentBracketView: React.FC = () => {\n  const [tournaments, setTournaments] = useState<TournamentRecord[]>([]);\n  const [activeTourneyId, setActiveTourneyId] = useState<string | loadProjection = async (id: string) => {\n    setIsLoading(true);\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`/api/tournaments/${id}`);\n      if (res.ok) {\n        const data: TournamentProjection = await res.json();\n        setProjection(data);\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('[Tournament] Failed to load bracket projection:', e);\n    } finally {\n      setIsLoading(false);\n   const el = container.querySelector(`.bracket-slot-${slotClass}`);\n      if (!el) return null;\n      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const c = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      return {\n        x: r.left - c.left + r.width / 2,\n        y: r.top - c.top + r.height / 2,\n      };\n    };\n\n    const paths: Array<{ d: string; isActive: boolean }> = [];\n\n    const connectNodes = (aClass: string, bClass: string, parentClass: string) => {\n      const a = getNodeCenter(aClass);\n      const b = getNodeCenter(bClass);\n      const p = getNodeCenter(parentClass);\n      if (!p) return;\n\n      if (a) {\n        const midXA = (a.x + p.x) / 2;\n        paths.push({\n          d: `M ${a.x} ${a.y} H ${midXA} V ${p.y} H ${p.x}`,\n          isActive: Boolean(projection?.slots_map[parentClass]?.character_id),\n        });\n      }\n      if (b) {\n        const midXB = (b.x + p.x) / 2;\n        paths.push({\n          d: `M ${b.x} ${b.y} H ${midXB} V ${p.y} H ${p.x}`,\n          isActive: Boolean(projection?.slots_map[parentClass]?.character_id),\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    // Left Wing\n    connectNodes('l1', 'l2', 'l9');\n    connectNodes('l3', 'l4', 'l10');\n    connectNodes('l5', 'l6', 'l11');\n    connectNodes('l7', 'l8', 'l12');\n\n    connectNodes('l9', 'l10', 'l13');\n    connectNodes('l11', 'l12', 'l14');\n\n    connectNodes('l13', 'l14', 'l15');\n\n    // Right Wing\n    connectNodes('r1', 'r2', 'r9');\n    connectNodes('r3', 'r4', 'r10');\n    connectNodes('r5', 'r6', 'r11');\n    connectNodes('r7', 'r8', connectNodes('l15', 'r15', 'champion');\n\n    setSvgPaths(paths);\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const timer = setTimeout(updateConnectors, 100);\n    window.addEventListener('resize', const handlePickWinner = async (winnerId: string) => {\n    if (!activeTourneyId || !selectedMatch) return;\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(\n        `/api/tournaments/${activeTourneyId}/match/${selectedMatch.id}/winner`,\n        {\n          method: 'POST',\n          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n          body:    }\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('[Tournament] Failed to record winner:', e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // Create New Tournament\n  const handleCreateTournament = async () => {\n    if       isWinner ? 'node-winner' : hasData ? '' : 'node-empty'\n        }`}\n      >\n        {slot ? {"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\wow-i-ll-say-that-bright-blum.md: # Rebased Character Identity SQLite Engine & Tournament Auto-Suggest — Implementation Plan\n\n## Context\nThe Manager requested moving Character Identity from flat JSON files (`Hazard_Trigger_Data/Identity/Characters/TT01/A01.json`) into an authoritative relational SQLite database following the **Entropy Rose** architectural style. In tournaments, rather than typing arbitrary names or creating ad-hoc entries, the Manager wants to specify competitors solely by their **Character ID** via an **auto-suggest selector** (with options strictly constrained to existing character IDs in the SQLite database, with \"no create new\").\n\n---\n\n## 1. Schema & Hex ID Design (Rose Relational Pattern)\n\n### Hex ID Prefix Standards (`<System \"1\"><3-Hex-Type><12-Hex-Timestamp>`)\n- `1001` — Tournaments\n- `1002` — Tournament Matches / Bracket Slots\n- `1003` — Tournament Participants / Seeds\n- **`1004`** — Characters (Identity Entities)\n- **`1005`** — Character Variants / Outfits\n\n### Relational Schema Definition (`backend/character_db.py`)\n\n```sql\n-- Table: characters (1004...)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS characters (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- 1004... 16-character monotonic hex ID\n    char_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,      -- Character Code e.g. 'A01', 'B28', 'B53', 'C14'\n    canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,        -- Primary Name e.g. 'Amelia Watson'\n    series TEXT,                         -- World / Series / Franchise e.g. 'Hololive', 'Azur Lane'\n    associate_json TEXT,                 -- Association array e.g. '[\"HoloMyth\"]'\n    names_json TEXT,                     -- { names: [...], nicknames: [...], aliases: [...] }\n    hazard_score REAL DEFAULT 0.0,       -- Plain decimal odometer <a.b> (e.g. 3.0)\n    hazard_class TEXT,                   -- Class I..V classification badge\n    base_prompt TEXT,                    -- _prpt_ visual token string\n    outfit TEXT,                         -- Default outfit token string\n    avatar_url TEXT,                     -- Image/Avatar URL or relative path\n    taxonomy_json TEXT,                  -- { archetype: [], personalityTropes: [], addressment: [] }\n    dna_phase1_json TEXT,                -- Physical traits: hair, eyes, physique\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_characters_char_code ON characters(char_code);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_characters_name ON characters(canonical_name);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_characters_series ON characters(series);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_characters_hazard ON characters(hazard_score);\n\n-- Table: character_variants (1005...)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS character_variants (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- 1005... 16-character monotonic hex ID\n    character_id TEXT NOT NULL,          -- Foreign key to characters(id)\n    variant_key TEXT NOT NULL,           -- e.g. 'default', 'ego_01', 'skin_01'\n    variant_name TEXT NOT NULL,          -- e.g. 'The Casual Ame'\n    prompt TEXT,                         -- Variant prompt tokens\n    outfit TEXT,                         -- Variant outfit tokens\n    is_default INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    FOREIGN KEY (character_id) REFERENCES characters(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n    UNIQUE(character_id, variant_key)\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cv_char ON character_variants(character_id);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ingestion & Roster Seeding\n\n### Ingestion Source:\n1. `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Rebased\\data\\Hazard_Trigger_Data\\Identity\\Characters\\TT01\\A01.json`\n   - Ingests `A01` (Amelia Watson) and variant `ego_01` (The Casual Ame).\n2. Archive Roster from `Tournament 01 ~ Genesis` and `Tournament 02 ~ Duality`:\n   - Pre-seeds character identities for tournament competitors (`A20 Topaz`, `A27 Nico Robin`, `A68 Silver Wolf`, `B02 Amane Kanata`, `B05 Usada Pekora`, `B28 Hino Akane`, `B53 Alabama`, `B56 Wang Liu Mei`, `B57 Lucia`, `C14 Kirishima Romin`, `C17 Friend A`, `C25 Yonah`, `EVA_01 Eva Frontier`, etc.).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Backend REST API Endpoints (`backend/main.py`)\n\n- `GET /api/characters?q=<query>&limit=20`:\n  - Returns matching characters ordered by `char_code ASC` for live auto-suggest.\n  - Returns `{ char_code, canonical_name, series, avatar_url, hazard_score, hazard_class }`.\n- `GET /api/characters/{char_code}`:\n  - Returns full character record and all variants.\n- `POST /api/characters`:\n  - Upserts character definition into SQLite.\n- `PUT /api/tournaments/{tournament_id}/slot/{slot_code}/select-character`:\n  - Accepts `{ char_code: str }`.\n  - Looks up `char_code` in SQLite `characters` table.\n  - Sets `character_id`, `display_name`, and `avatar_url` on the slot.\n  - Refuses assignment if `char_code` does not exist in SQLite.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Frontend Auto-Suggest Character Selector (`frontend/src/app/components/CharacterIdSelector.tsx`)\n\n### UI/UX Specifications:\n- **Search-as-you-type Dropdown**:\n  - Focus input: Typing `A` reveals `A01 - Amelia Watson`, `A20 - Topaz`, `A68 - Silver Wolf`.\n  - Up/Down arrow keyboard navigation, `Enter` to select, `Escape` to close.\n  - Shows Avatar thumbnail, `char_code` badge in gold (`A01`), Name, Series, and Hazard Class pill.\n- **Strict \"No Create New\" Constraint**:\n  - Only valid existing `char_code`s from the SQLite database can be selected.\n  - If a code doesn't exist, displays a red border and `<span className=\"text-hazard-crimson\">Character ID not found in database</span>`.\n- **Integration**:\n  1. **New Bracket Wizard**:\n     - All 16 slots (`l1..l8`, `r1..r8`) use `CharacterIdSelector`.\n     - In Bulk Paste mode: Pasting lines of IDs (e.g. `A01\\nB28\\nB53`) resolves each code against the SQLite character registry.\n  2. **In-Bracket Slot Quick Edit**:\n     - Clicking any base slot opens `CharacterIdSelector` to quickly reassign that slot to any registered character.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Implementation Steps\n\n1. **Step 1: Backend Database (`backend/character_db.py`)**\n   - Create tables `characters` and `character_variants` with Hex ID generation (`1004...`, `1005...`).\n   - Implement `search_characters(query, limit)` and `get_character(char_code)`.\n   - Implement `ingest_character_json(file_path)` to ingest `A01.json`.\n   - Seed Genesis & Duality character rosters into SQLite.\n\n2. **Step 2: API Endpoints (`backend/main.py`)**\n   - Add `/api/characters` and `/api/characters/{char_code}` routes.\n   - Add slot assignment endpoint by `char_code`.\n\n3. **Step 3: Frontend Character Selector (`frontend/src/app/components/CharacterIdSelector.tsx`)**\n   - Implement search-as-you-type input with debounced API queries.\n   - Render dropdown options with avatar, `char_code`, name, and hazard score.\n   - Enforce \"no create new\" validation.\n\n4. **Step 4: Connect to Tournament Bracket Studio**\n   - Wire `CharacterIdSelector` into `TournamentBracketView.tsx` for both the New Bracket Wizard and live slot editing.\n\n5. **Step 5: End-to-End Verification**\n   - Test search query `A01` returns Amelia Watson.\n   - Test selecting `A01` seeds slot `l1` with avatar and name.\n   - Test invalid code rejection.\n"}

{\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(value || '');\n  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [results, setResults] = useState<CharacterSearchResult[]>([]);\n  const [isLoading, with debounce\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    const fetchMatches = async () => {\n      setIsLoading(true);\n      try {\n        const res = await fetch(`/api/characters?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=15`);\n        if (res.ok && active) {\n          const data: CharacterSearchResult[] = await res.json();\n          setResults(data);\n          setSelectedIndex(0);\n\n          // Validate if exact match exists when query is non-empty\n          if (query.trim()) {\n            const hasExact = data.some(\n              (c) => } catch (e) {\n        console.error('[CharacterIdSelector] Search error:', e);\n      } finally {\n        if (active) setIsLoading(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const timer = document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleSelect = (char: CharacterSearchResult) => {\n    setQuery(char.char_code);\n    setIsValidMatch(true);\n    setIsOpen(false);\n    onSelect(char);\n  };\n\n  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {\n    if (!isOpen) {\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n        setIsOpen(true);\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      setSelectedIndex((prev) => (prev + 1 < results.length ? prev + 1 : 0));\n    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      setSelectedIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 >= 0 ? prev - 1 : results.length - 1));\n    } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (results[selectedIndex]) {\n        handleSelect(results[selectedIndex]);\n      }\n    } else if (e.key Current Character API Endpoints (main.py)\n  \n  | Endpoint | Method | Purpose |\n  |----------|--------|---------|\n  | `/api/characters` | GET | Search/list characters (`?q=&amp;limit=`) |\n  | `/api/characters/{char_code}` | GET | Get full character + variants |\n  | `/api/characters` | POST | Upsert character (with variants) |\n  | `/api/tournaments/{tid}/slot/{slot}/select-character` | PUT | Assign existing character to tournament slot |\n  \n  **Search behavior** (from `character_db.py:search_characters`):\n  - Matches `char_code` (prefix priority), `canonical_name`, `series`\n  - Returns: `id`, `char_code`, `canonical_name`, `series`, `avatar_url`, `hazard_score`, `hazard_class`\n  \n  ---\n  \n  ### 4. CharacterIdSelector Frontend Component\n  \n  **Location:** `frontend/src/app/components/CharacterIdSelector.tsx`\n  \n  **Props:**\n  ```typescript\n  interface CharacterIdSelectorProps {\n    value: string;           // char_code (e.g., 'A01')\n    displayName?: string;    // Resolved canonical name\n    avatarUrl?: string;\n    onSelect: (character: CharacterSearchResult) =&gt; void;\n    placeholder?: string;\n    disabled?: boolean;\n  }\n  ```\n  \n  **Features:**\n  - Debounced search (150ms) against `/api/characters?q=`\n  - Strict validation: only pre-registered IDs allowed (shows \"Not in DB\" badge)\n  - Keyboard navigation (ArrowUp/Down, Enter, Escape)\n  - Displays: avatar, char_code badge, canonical_name, series, hazard score pill\n  - Click-outside closes dropdown\n  - Loading/error/empty states\n  \n  **Search Result Type:**\n  ```typescript\n  interface CharacterSearchResult {\n    id: string;\n    char_code: string;\n    canonical_name: string;\n    series: string;\n    avatar_url?: string;\n    hazard_score?: number;\n    hazard_class?: string;\n  }\n  ```\n  \n  ---\n  \n  ### 5. Rose's 5-Tier Pattern Mapping\n  \n  **Rose's 5-Tier Relational Pattern:**\n  | Tier | Concept | Current State |\n  |------|---------|---------------|\n  | **1. Quotes → Characters** | Characters are quote speakers | ✅ `characters` table exists with `canonical_name`, `char_code` |\n  | **2. Containers → Series/Worlds** | Series as containers | ⚠ `series` is a TEXT field, not a relational table |\n  | **3. Specifics → Variants** | Variants/outfits as specifics | ✅ `character_variants` table exists (1005...) |\n  | **4. Themes → Taxonomy** | Taxonomy tags as themes | ⚠ `taxonomy_json` blob - not relational |\n  | **5. Usages → Physical Traits** | Physical traits from prompts | ❌ **MISSING** - no trait extraction from `_prpt_` |\n  \n  ---\n  \n  ### Gap Analysis: What's Missing for Full 5-Tier Alignment\n  \n  | Tier | Missing / Needs Work |\n  |------|---------------------|\n  | **1. Characters** | ✅ Mostly complete. Could add: `display_name`, `description`, `birth_date`, `height`, `voice_actor` |\n  | **2. Series/Worlds** | ❌ Need `series` table (id, name, franchise, universe, media_type) with FK from characters |\n  | **3. Variants** | ✅ Exists. Could add: `trigger_conditions`, `rarity`, `release_date`, `artist` |\n  | **4. Taxonomy** | ❌ Need relational tables: `taxonomy_tags` (id, category, tag), `character_taxonomy` (character_id, tag_id) with categories: `archetype`, `personality_trope`, `addressment`, `role`, `alignment` |\n  | **5. Physical Traits** | ❌ **Critical gap** - Need to parse `_prpt_` prompts into structured traits: `hair_color`, `hair_style`, `eye_color`, `body_type`, `clothing_items`, `accessories`, `pose`, `expression`. Could use `dna_phase1_json` column for this |\n  \n  ---\n  \n  ### Recommended Schema Extensions for 5-Tier Alignment\n  \n  ```sql\n  -- Tier 2: Series/Worlds container\n  CREATE TABLE series_worlds (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,           -- e.g., \"SER_HOLOLIVE\"\n      name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n      franchise TEXT,                -- \"Hololive\", \"Honkai: Star Rail\"\n      universe TEXT,                 -- \"VTuber\", \"Sci-Fi\", \"Fantasy\"\n      media_type TEXT,               -- \"VTuber\", \"Game\", \"Anime\", \"Manga\"\n      description TEXT,\n      created_at TEXT NOT NULL\n  );\n  \n  -- Tier 4: Taxonomy (themes) - normalized\n  CREATE TABLE taxonomy_tags (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      category TEXT NOT NULL,        -- 'archetype', 'personality_trope', 'addressment', 'role', 'alignment'\n      tag TEXT NOT NULL,\n      description TEXT,\n      UNIQUE(category, tag)\n  );\n  \n  CREATE TABLE character_taxonomy (\n      character_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      tag_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      PRIMARY KEY (character_id, tag_id),\n      FOREIGN KEY (character_id) REFERENCES characters(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n      FOREIGN KEY (tag_id) REFERENCES taxonomy_tags(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n  );\n  \n  -- Tier 5: Physical Traits (usages) - extracted from prompts\n  CREATE TABLE physical_traits (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      character_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      trait_category TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'hair', 'eyes', 'body', 'clothing', 'accessory', 'pose', 'expression'\n      trait_key TEXT NOT NULL,       -- e.g., 'blonde', 'bob_cut', 'blue', 'thighhigh', 'deerstalker'\n      trait_value TEXT,              -- Optional value/detail\n      source TEXT DEFAULT 'prompt',  -- 'prompt', 'variant', 'manual'\n      confidence REAL DEFAULT 1.0,\n      FOREIGN KEY (character_id) REFERENCES characters(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n  );\n  CREATE INDEX idx_physical_traits_char ON physical_traits(character_id);\n  CREATE INDEX idx_physical_traits_cat ON physical_traits(trait_category);\n  ```\n  \n  ---\n  \n  ### Summary\n  \n  | Aspect | Status |\n  |--------|--------|\n  | Character JSON parsing | ✅ Working (ingest_character_json) |\n  | SQLite schema (1004/1005) | ✅ Basic structure exists |\n  | API endpoints | ✅ CRUD + search implemented |\n  | Frontend selector | ✅ Full-featured with validation |\n  | **5-Tier alignment** | **Partial (2/5 tiers properly relational)** |\n  \n  **Priority for full alignment:**\n  1. **Tier 5 (Physical Traits)** - Prompt Token Extraction Pipeline\n\n### Extraction Strategy\n1. **Parse `_prpt_` strings** from `default` and all variants\n2. **Tokenize** by comma, handle quoted phrases\n3. **Normalize** tokens (lowercase, trim, handle synonyms)\n4. **Match** against `trait_options.trait_key` (with fuzzy/fuzzywuzzy fallback)\n5. **Assign** to `physical_traits` with confidence scoring\n6. **Handle conflicts** using `layer_conflicts` and `visibility_conditions`\n\n### Extraction Flow\n```python\ndef extract_traits_from_prompt(prompt: str, character_id: str, variant_id: str = None):\n    tokens = normalize_tokens(prompt.split(','))\n    for token in tokens:\n        match = find_best_trait_option(token)\n        if match:\n            confidence = calculate_confidence(token, match)\n            upsert_physical_trait(character_id, match.id, variant_id, confidence)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Ingestion Pipeline\n\n### Source Files\n1. **Characters**: `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Identity/Characters/TT01/A01.json` (and future TT02, TT03...)\n2. **Topology** (physical traits taxonomy):\n   - `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Topology/Tree/_1_groups.json` → `trait_categories`\n   - `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Topology/Tree/_1_physical_appearance.json` → `trait_options`\n   - `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Topology/Tables/_2_condition_affect.json` → `visibility_conditions`\n   - `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Topology/Tables/_3_condition_layer.json` → `layer_conflicts`\n   - `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Topology/Tables/_4_condition_construct.json` → `composite_constructs`\n\n### Ingestion Order\n1. **Tier 5**: Load topology → `trait_categories`, `trait_options`, `visibility_conditions`, `layer_conflicts`, `composite_constructs`\n2. **Tier 2**: Extract series from `associate` fields → `series_worlds`\n3. **Tier 1**: Ingest characters from JSON → `characters` + `character_variants`\n4. **Tier 4**: Parse `taxonomy` fields → `taxonomy_tags` + `character_taxonomy`\n5. **Tier 5**: Parse `_prpt_` from all variants → `physical_traits`\n\n---\n\n## 6. {"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\wow-i-ll-say-that-bright-blum.md: # Rebased Character Identity SQLite Engine — Simplified 3-Table Core\n\n## Context\nThe Manager wants a minimal, pragmatic character database with just **3 core tables**:\n1. **Characters** — original characters with their default build\n2. **Variants** — other outfits/versions of each character\n3. **Worlds/Series** — franchise/world groupings\n\nEverything else (taxonomy, physical traits, topology, extraction) is deferred.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Three Core Tables (Rose 3-Tier Core)\n\n### Table 1: Characters (1004...) — Original Characters\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS characters (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- 1004... 16-char monotonic hex\n    char_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,            -- 'A01', 'B28', 'B53'\n    canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,              -- 'Amelia Watson'\n    display_name TEXT,                         -- optional override\n    description TEXT,                          -- bio/lore\n    series_id TEXT,                            -- FK → worlds(id)\n    names_json TEXT NOT NULL,                  -- {names:[], nicknames:[], aliases:[]}\n    associate_json TEXT,                       -- ['HoloMyth']\n    hazard_score REAL DEFAULT 0.0,             -- 0.0-10.0\n    hazard_class TEXT,                         -- 'Class I - Minimal Risk'\n    base_prompt TEXT,                          -- _prpt_ from default\n    outfit TEXT,                               -- default outfit string\n    avatar_url TEXT,                           -- image URL\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    FOREIGN KEY (series_id) REFERENCES worlds(id) ON DELETE SET NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX idx_characters_char_code ON characters(char_code);\nCREATE INDEX idx_characters_name ON characters(canonical_name);\nCREATE INDEX idx_characters_series ON characters(series_id);\n```\n\n### Table 2: Variants (1005...) — Other Outfits/Versions\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS variants (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- 1005... prefix\n    character_id TEXT NOT NULL,                -- FK → characters(id)\n    variant_key TEXT NOT NULL,                 -- 'ego_01', 'skin_01'\n    variant_name TEXT NOT NULL,                -- 'The Casual Ame'\n    prompt TEXT,                               -- _prpt_ variant prompt\n    outfit TEXT,                               -- variant outfit string\n    is_default INTEGER DEFAULT 0,              -- 1 if default build\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    FOREIGN KEY (character_id) REFERENCES characters(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n    UNIQUE(character_id, variant_key)\n);\nCREATE INDEX idx_variants_char ON variants(character_id);\n```\n\n### Table 3: Worlds/Series — Franchise Groupings\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS worlds (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- e.g. 'WLD_HOLOLIVE'\n    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,                 -- 'Hololive'\n    franchise TEXT,                            -- 'Hololive'\n    universe TEXT,                             -- 'VTuber', 'Sci-Fi', 'Fantasy'\n    media_type TEXT,                           -- 'VTuber', 'Game', 'Anime'\n    description TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX idx_worlds_name ON worlds(name);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ingestion Scope (Minimal)\n\n### Source Data\n- **Characters**: `data/Hazard_Trigger_Data/Identity/Characters/TT01/A01.json`\n- **Worlds**: Extracted from `associate` field in character JSONs\n\n### Ingestion Order\n1. **Worlds** — extract from `associate` field in all character JSONs\n2. **Characters** — ingest `default` block from each character JSON\n3. **Variants** — ingest each non-default key (e.g., `ego_01`) as variant\n\n### Mapping from A01.json\n| JSON Field | → Table | Column |\n|------------|---------|--------|\n| `default.id` | characters | `char_code` |\n| `default.names.names[0]` | characters | `canonical_name` |\n| `default.names` | characters | `names_json` |\n| `default.associate` | characters | `series_id` (FK to worlds) |\n| `default._prpt_` | characters | `base_prompt` |\n| `default.outfit` | characters | `outfit` |\n| `default.hazardLevel.hazardScore` | characters | `hazard_score` |\n| `ego_01` key | variants | `variant_key = 'ego_01'` |\n| `ego_01._prpt_` | variants | `prompt` |\n| `ego_01.outfit` | variants | `outfit` |\n| `ego_01.names[0]` | variants | `variant_name` |\n\n---\n\n## 3.
